# ADR 0012, and the table's own rule (EventBindings.cpp `LegacyEventInterface`):
# a legacy name is in the table only when this browser has the interface behind
# it. `DeviceMotionEvent` and `DeviceOrientationEvent` are refused twice over --
# there is no sensor and ADR 0029 says a table entry would be a fingerprinting
# surface. `BeforeUnloadEvent`, `TextEvent` and `StorageEvent` name events this
# browser never fires, and a name that answers is what sends a page down the
# native path. Deliberate; do not "fix" by adding the aliases.
[dom/nodes/Document-createEvent.https.html]
FAIL=BEFOREUNLOADEVENT should be an alias for BeforeUnloadEvent.
FAIL=BeforeUnloadEvent should be an alias for BeforeUnloadEvent.
FAIL=DEVICEMOTIONEVENT should be an alias for DeviceMotionEvent.
FAIL=DEVICEORIENTATIONEVENT should be an alias for DeviceOrientationEvent.
FAIL=DeviceMotionEvent should be an alias for DeviceMotionEvent.
FAIL=DeviceOrientationEvent should be an alias for DeviceOrientationEvent.
FAIL=STORAGEEVENT should be an alias for StorageEvent.
FAIL=StorageEvent should be an alias for StorageEvent.
FAIL=TEXTEVENT should be an alias for TextEvent.
PRECONDITION_FAILED=TOUCHEVENT should be an alias for TouchEvent.
FAIL=TextEvent should be an alias for TextEvent.
PRECONDITION_FAILED=TouchEvent should be an alias for TouchEvent.
FAIL=beforeunloadevent should be an alias for BeforeUnloadEvent.
FAIL=createEvent('BEFOREUNLOADEVENT') should be initialized correctly.
FAIL=createEvent('BeforeUnloadEvent') should be initialized correctly.
FAIL=createEvent('DEVICEMOTIONEVENT') should be initialized correctly.
FAIL=createEvent('DEVICEORIENTATIONEVENT') should be initialized correctly.
FAIL=createEvent('DeviceMotionEvent') should be initialized correctly.
FAIL=createEvent('DeviceOrientationEvent') should be initialized correctly.
FAIL=createEvent('STORAGEEVENT') should be initialized correctly.
FAIL=createEvent('StorageEvent') should be initialized correctly.
FAIL=createEvent('TEXTEVENT') should be initialized correctly.
PRECONDITION_FAILED=createEvent('TOUCHEVENT') should be initialized correctly.
FAIL=createEvent('TextEvent') should be initialized correctly.
PRECONDITION_FAILED=createEvent('TouchEvent') should be initialized correctly.
FAIL=createEvent('beforeunloadevent') should be initialized correctly.
FAIL=createEvent('devicemotionevent') should be initialized correctly.
FAIL=createEvent('deviceorientationevent') should be initialized correctly.
FAIL=createEvent('storageevent') should be initialized correctly.
FAIL=createEvent('textevent') should be initialized correctly.
PRECONDITION_FAILED=createEvent('touchevent') should be initialized correctly.
FAIL=devicemotionevent should be an alias for DeviceMotionEvent.
FAIL=deviceorientationevent should be an alias for DeviceOrientationEvent.
FAIL=storageevent should be an alias for StorageEvent.
FAIL=textevent should be an alias for TextEvent.
PRECONDITION_FAILED=touchevent should be an alias for TouchEvent.

# Attributes on a *processing instruction* are WICG/declarative-partial-updates,
# not the DOM: the file's own `link rel=help` points at the proposal. No engine
# ships it and the DOM gives a ProcessingInstruction no `getAttribute` at all.
# ADR 0012's rule, the same one OpaqueRange gets: an API a page could feature-
# detect is worse present-and-approximate than absent. 126 subtests, and they
# are the largest single block of "failures" in dom/nodes that is not a bug.

# **`Observable` is refused, and every failure under dom/observable/ below is
# that one decision.** ADR 0012: WPT keeps it under `tentative/` because it is
# a proposal -- not standardised, and no engine ships it. `EventTarget.when()`
# and the whole operator set are exactly the shape that ADR warns about: a page
# that feature-detects `Observable` and finds a partial one has no fallback
# path left, where a page that finds nothing loads a library that works.
# Implementing it is a change to ADR 0012, which is a separate commit and a
# separate argument. 251 subtests, and they are not bugs.

# TD-0052: this file declares `?mode=open` and `?mode=closed` variants, and the
# runner does not expand `<meta name="variant">` for a plain HTML test -- so it
# runs bare, `(new URLSearchParams(location.search)).get("mode")` is null, and
# the test calls `attachShadow({mode: null})`. **A correct engine throws there**:
# `mode` is a required `ShadowRootMode` and null is not one of its two values.
# This passed until 2026-08-12 only because `attachShadow` treated anything that
# was not "closed" as "open". Nothing about Ranges in shadow trees is broken;
# fixing the harness is what makes this measurable, not fixing `dom` or `bindings`.
